(91)

Here we have used bars to denote the so called odd parts of the decompositions, whilst the even parts are written without bars. In the following, when considering the relations for the individual harmonic coefficients, we will omit their subscripts, as this should not lead to any ambiguities.

Inserting the harmonic decompositions into the linearized equations and using the properties of the harmonics, described in appendix
B
, the system can be reduced significantly by solving for some of the harmonic coefficients in terms of the others. The harmonic decompositions of the linearized equations, which were presented in Sec.
V
, can be found in appendix
C
. However, here we encounter a difficulty which was not present in previous papers. At this point in previous works, the system effectively divides into two separate parts, one containing the odd coefficients and another containing the even parts
Bradley et al. 2017
;
Törnkvist and Bradley 2019
;
Semrén and Bradley 2022
. One exception to the division into even and odd equations, which is also present in previous works, are quantities defined using a Levi-Civita psuedo-tensor, such as the vorticity and the magnetic part of the Weyl tensor. For these quantities it is generally the even coefficients that are present in the odd system and vice versa, but the systems still decouple from each other. However, when including a non-zero magnetic field to zeroth order, the division into even and odd subsystems does not seem possible in general, as the pseudoscalar
\mathfrak{B}
serves as a coupling that connects the systems together. This can be seen when considering the harmonically decomposed momentum equations for the fluid components

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left(\mu_{(i)}+p_{(i)}\right)\dot{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}=&-\dot{p}_{(i)}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}-p^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\rho_{(i)}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{V}-\mathfrak{B}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\right)\\
~&-\left(\mu_{(i)}+p_{(i)}\right)\left(\mathcal{A}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\right)+\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},\end{split}

(92)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\left(\mu_{(i)}+p_{(i)}\right)\dot{\overline{\mathcal{V}}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}=&-\dot{p}_{(i)}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}-\overline{p}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\rho_{(i)}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}+\mathfrak{B}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\right)\\
~&-\left(\mu_{(i)}+p_{(i)}\right)\left(\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\right)+\overline{\mathcal{F}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}.\end{split}

(93)

Here it can be explicitly observed that
\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}
is coupled to
\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}
through the terms proportional to
\mathfrak{B}
. To avoid this problem and simplify matters even further, we will now apply the cold MHD approximation

VII
Cold MHD Approximation

In the cold MHD approximation, we assume that
\mu_{(i)}\approx m_{(i)}\mathcal{N}_{(i)}
and
p_{(i)}\approx 0
to both zeroth and first order, and write the harmonic equations in terms of the collective variables

\displaystyle\mu_{(F)}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{i}\mu_{(i)},\quad

\displaystyle\mu_{(F)}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{S}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{i}\mu_{(i)}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{S}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},

\displaystyle\mu^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{i}\mu^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},\quad

\displaystyle\mu_{(F)}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{i}\mu_{(i)}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},

(94)

\displaystyle\overline{\mu}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{i}\overline{\mu}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},\quad

\displaystyle\mu_{(F)}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{i}\mu_{(i)}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.07932pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4992pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.92802pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(i)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}.

The equations governing these variables are found by adding the equations for the individual fluid components. Using charge neutrality to zeroth order and assuming that the plasma consists of electrons and a much more massive ion counterpart, it can then be shown that the collective velocities satisfy the equations

\displaystyle\mu_{(F)}\dot{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}=

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathfrak{B}}{\eta}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}+\mathfrak{B}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\right)-\mu_{(F)}\left(\mathcal{A}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\right),

(95)

\displaystyle~ \mu_{(F)}\dot{\overline{\mathcal{V}}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}=

\displaystyle~\frac{\mathfrak{B}}{\eta}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{V}-\mathfrak{B}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\right)-\mu_{(F)}\left(\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\right),

(96)

where we have written the currents as

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{V}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\eta}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{V}-\mathfrak{B}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\right),

(97)

\displaystyle~\overline{\mathcal{J}}^{V}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\eta}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}+\mathfrak{B}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\right),

(98)

using the linearised and harmonically decomposed version of Eq. (
23
). The other equations obtained on rewriting the multifluid system in this manner can be found in appendix
D
. The momentum equations, Eqs. (
95
)–(
96
), should be compared to Eqs. (
92
)–(
93
). At the cost of introducing an electrical resistivity
\eta
, we see that we no longer have any coupling between the even and odd velocities in Eqs. (
95
)–(
96
), in contrast to Eqs. (
92
)–(
93
). This simplifies matters, and the system is ultimately seen to decouple into two subsectors, as shown in Sec.
VIII
. However, we will need some model for the electrical resistivity if we want to completely close the system. Therefore, when performing numerical calculations in Sec.
IX
, we will make the simplified assumption that
\eta
follows a Spitzer-like expression so that

\eta\propto{T_{(e)}}^{-3/2},

(99)

where
T_{(e)}
is the electron temperature
Baumjohann and Treumann 2012
. Assuming that the electron pressure, although neglected in the main equations, follows a polytropic equation of state and an ideal gas law,

p_{(e)}\propto{\mathcal{N}_{(e)}}^{\gamma}\propto\mathcal{N}_{(e)}T_{(e)},

(100)

it follows from the electron number conservation and Eq. (
99
) that

\dot{\eta}=\frac{3\left(\gamma-1\right)}{2}\Theta\eta.

(101)

VIII
Final System

The harmonically decomposed system of equations in the cold MHD approximation can be significantly reduced by solving for some of the coefficients in terms of the others. However, before arriving at the final system, we should note that there is still some freedom in specifying the dyad
\{u^{a},n^{a}\}
to first order. To zeroth order, this dyad is fixed. The vector field
u^{a}
is then assumed to be orthogonal to the hypersurfaces of homogeneity, and coincides with the plasma velocities. As for the field
n^{a}
, this is fixed since it defines the direction of the local rotational symmetry. However, when going to first order, the homogeneity and rotational symmetry are broken, and the vector field
u^{a}
does not need to coincide with the plasma velocities. As a result, there is some freedom in defining
u^{a}
and
n^{a}
to first order. We will use this freedom to set
a^{V}
,
\overline{a}^{V}
,
\mathcal{A}^{S}
,
\mathcal{A}^{V}
, and
\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{V}
to zero. For details on the effect and use of infinitesimal transformations of the dyad
\{u^{a},n^{a}\}
, the reader is referred to
Keresztes et al. 2015
.

Finally, after specifying the dyad to first order, we arrive at two subsystems for the first order quantities. These systems are seen to close independently of each other and consist of the odd sector
\{\Omega^{S},\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T},\mathcal{H}^{T},{\overline{\mathcal{V}}_{(F)}}^{V},\mathfrak{E}^{S},\mathfrak{E}^{V},\overline{\mathfrak{B}}^{V}\}
, and the even sector
\{\overline{\Omega}^{V},\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T},\mathcal{E}^{T},\Sigma^{T},{\mu_{(F)}}^{V}
,
{\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{S},{\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{V},\mathcal{B}^{V},\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}\}
. All other coefficients can be given algebraically in terms of these sets.

VIII.1
Odd Sector

The coefficients in the odd sector satisfy the following evolution equations

\displaystyle\dot{\Omega}^{S}=

\displaystyle~\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\Omega^{S},

(102)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\dot{\overline{\mathcal{E}}}^{T}=&-\frac{3}{2}\left(F+\Sigma D\right)\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\left(1-D\right)\mathcal{H}^{T}\\
&+P\Omega^{S}+\frac{1}{a_{2}}\left(1+D\right)\left({\mu_{(F)}}{\overline{\mathcal{V}}_{(F)}}^{V}+\mathfrak{B}\mathfrak{E}^{V}\right),\end{split}

(103)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\dot{\mathcal{H}}^{T}=&~\left(\frac{3ia_{1}\Pi}{\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)+S\right)\Omega^{S}+\frac{1}{a_{2}}\mathfrak{B}\overline{\mathfrak{B}}^{V}\\
~&+\frac{ia_{1}}{a_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}B}\Bigg[\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\left(3\Pi+\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}\right)\\
~ &-\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\Bigg]\left({\mu_{(F)}}{\overline{\mathcal{V}}_{(F)}}^{V}+\mathfrak{B}\mathfrak{E}^{V}\right)\\
~&+\frac{ia_{1}}{2\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\left(\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}-CB+9\Sigma E+3\Pi\right)\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}-\frac{3}{2}\left(2E+F\right)\mathcal{H}^{T},\end{split}

(104)

\displaystyle~\begin{split}{\dot{\overline{\mathcal{V}}}_{(F)}}^{V}=&~\frac{\mathfrak{B}}{\eta{\mu_{(F)}}}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{V}-\mathfrak{B}{\overline{\mathcal{V}}_{(F)}}^{V}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right){\overline{\mathcal{V}}_{(F)}}^{V},\end{split}

(105)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\dot{\mathfrak{E}}^{S}=&~\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}}\overline{\mathfrak{B}}^{V}-\frac{2ia_{1}}{\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\mathfrak{B}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\Omega^{S}+\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}-\frac{1}{\eta}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{S},\end{split}

(106)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\dot{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}=&~\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\overline{\mathfrak{B}}^{V}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma+\frac{4\Theta}{3}\right)\mathfrak{E}^{V}-\frac{1}{\eta}\left(\mathfrak{E}^{V}-\mathfrak{B}{\overline{\mathcal{V}}_{(F)}}^{V}\right)-\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\mathfrak{B}}}{k_{\bot}^{2}}\Omega^{S},\end{split}

(107)

\displaystyle~\begin{split}\dot{\overline{\mathfrak{B}}}^{V}=&~\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\mathfrak{E}^{V}-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{S}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma+\frac{4\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\mathfrak{B}}^{V}\\
~&-\frac{ia_{1}}{\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\mathfrak{B}\Bigg[\frac{2\left(\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}\right)}{a_{2}B}\left(\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{T}\right)\\
~&-\frac{2}{B}\left(3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)+\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)\left({\mu_{(F)}}{\overline{\mathcal{V}}_{(F)}}^{V}+\mathfrak{B}\mathfrak{E}^{V}\right)\\
&-\frac{a_{2}}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\left(\mu+p-\frac{\Pi}{2}-3\mathcal{E}+2N+3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)+\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)\Omega^{S}\Bigg],\end{split}

(108)

where the auxiliary variables are defined as

\displaystyle\tilde{k}^{2}=

\displaystyle~\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}+\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}},

(109)

\displaystyle~ B=

\displaystyle~\tilde{k}^{2}+\frac{9}{2}\Sigma^{2}+3\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right),

(110)

\displaystyle~ CB=

\displaystyle~\Sigma\left(\Theta-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\right)-\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}^{2}},

(111)

\displaystyle~ F=

\displaystyle~\Sigma+\frac{2\Theta}{3},

(112)

\displaystyle~ D=

\displaystyle~C+\frac{\mu+p-2\Pi}{B},

(113)

\displaystyle~P=

\displaystyle~\frac{2}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}B}\left(\mu+p-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\left(B+3\mathcal{E}+\mu+p-\frac{1}{2}\Pi+\mathcal{R}-\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}\right),

(114)

\displaystyle~ S=

\displaystyle~\frac{2ia_{1}}{3\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}B}\left(\mu+p-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\left(3\Sigma\left(\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}-\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}+3\Pi\right)+\Theta\left(\tilde{k}^{2}+3\Pi\right)\right),

(115)

\displaystyle~ EB=

\displaystyle~\frac{\Sigma}{2}\left(CB-\mathcal{E}-\frac{5}{2}\Pi\right)+\frac{\Theta}{3}\left(\mathcal{E}-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right),

(116)

\displaystyle~ N=

\displaystyle~\left(\mu+p-\frac{1}{2}\Pi\right)\left(1+\frac{2}{a_{2}^{2}B}\left(\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}\right)\right).

(117)

We get the remaining relevant harmonic coefficients as algebraic expressions in terms of
\Omega^{S},\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T},\mathcal{H}^{T},{\overline{\mathcal{V}}_{(F)}}^{V},\mathfrak{E}^{S},\mathfrak{E}^{V}
, and
\overline{\mathfrak{B}}^{V}
. The remaining scalar coefficients are

\displaystyle\rho^{S}=

\displaystyle~\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\mathfrak{E}^{S}-\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}}\mathfrak{E}^{V}-2\mathfrak{B}\Omega^{S},\quad\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\xi^{S}=~\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\Omega^{S},

(118)

\displaystyle~\begin{split}\frac{ia_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\mathcal{H}^{S}=&~\frac{\left(\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}\right)}{a_{2}B}\left(\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{T}\right)-\frac{a_{2}}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\left(\mu+p-\frac{\Pi}{2}+N\right)\Omega^{S}\\
&-\frac{1}{B}\left(3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)+\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)\left(\mu_{(F)}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\mathfrak{B}\mathfrak{E}^{V}\right).\end{split}

(119)

The remaining vector coefficients are in turn given by

\displaystyle\Omega^{V}=

\displaystyle~\frac{ia_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}k_{\bot}^{2}}\Omega^{S},

(120)

\displaystyle~\overline{Q}^{V}=

\displaystyle~\mu_{(F)}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\mathfrak{B}\mathfrak{E}^{V},

(121)

\displaystyle~\overline{\mu}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}=

\displaystyle~\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\mu}_{(F)}}{k_{\bot}^{2}}\Omega^{S},\quad\overline{\mathcal{B}}^{V}=~\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\mathfrak{B}}}{k_{\bot}^{2}}\Omega^{S},\quad\overline{\mu}^{V}=~\overline{\mu}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\mathfrak{B}\overline{\mathcal{B}}^{V},

(122)

\displaystyle~\overline{W}^{V}=

\displaystyle~\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\Theta}}{k_{\bot}^{2}}\Omega^{S},\quad\overline{X}^{V}=~\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\mathcal{E}}}{k_{\bot}^{2}}\Omega^{S},\quad\overline{V}^{V}=~\frac{2a_{2}\dot{\Sigma}}{k_{\bot}^{2}}\Omega^{S},

(123)

\displaystyle~\overline{p}^{V}=

\displaystyle~\frac{\mathfrak{B}}{3}\overline{\mathcal{B}}^{V},\quad\overline{Y}^{V}=-\frac{4\mathfrak{B}}{3}\overline{\mathcal{B}}^{V},\quad\overline{\Pi}^{V}=-\mathfrak{B}\overline{\mathfrak{B}}^{V},

(124)

\displaystyle~\begin{split}\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\overline{\Sigma}^{V}=&-\frac{a_{2}}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\left(\mu+p-\frac{\Pi}{2}+N+3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)+\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)\Omega^{S}\\
&+\frac{(\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$})}{a_{2}B}\left(\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{T}\right)\\
&-\frac{1}{B}\left(3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)+\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)\left(\mu_{(F)}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\mathfrak{B}\mathfrak{E}^{V}\right),\end{split}

(125)

\displaystyle~\begin{split}\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{V}=&~\frac{ia_{1}a_{2}}{\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\Bigg(\left(3\mathcal{E}+\frac{\Pi}{2}+2\mu+2p\right)\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\\
&-\frac{3\Sigma}{B}\left(\mu+p-\frac{\Pi}{2}\right)\left(\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}+3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\right)\Bigg)\Omega^{S}\\
&-\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{2a_{2}}\left(\frac{ia_{1}}{\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\left(1-\frac{9\Sigma^{2}}{2B}\right)\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{3\Sigma}{B}\mathcal{H}^{T}\right)+\frac{\mathfrak{B}}{2}\overline{\mathfrak{B}}^{V}\\
&+\frac{ia_{1}}{\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}B}\Bigg[\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\left(\tilde{k}^{2}-\mathcal{R}-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\right)\\
~ &-\frac{3\Sigma\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}\Bigg]\left(\mu_{(F)}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\mathfrak{B}\mathfrak{E}^{V}\right),\end{split}

(126)

\displaystyle~\begin{split}\mathcal{H}^{V}=&~\frac{a_{2}}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\left(3\mathcal{E}-N\right)\Omega^{S}+\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}B}\left(\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{T}\right)\\
&-\frac{1}{B}\left(3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)+\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}-\frac{B}{2}\right)\left(\mu_{(F)}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\mathfrak{B}\mathfrak{E}^{V}\right),\end{split}

(127)

\displaystyle~\begin{split}\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\overline{\alpha}^{V}=&-\frac{a_{2}}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\left(3\mathcal{E}-N\right)\Omega^{S}-\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}B}\left(\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{T}\right)\\
&+\frac{1}{B}\left(3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)+\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)\left(\mu_{(F)}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\mathfrak{B}\mathfrak{E}^{V}\right).\end{split}

(128)

Finally, the algebraic tensor coefficients are

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{B}{2}\overline{\Sigma}^{T}=&-\frac{2}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\left(\mu+p+\frac{B-\Pi}{2}\right)\Omega^{S}+\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}+\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\mathcal{H}^{T}\\
~&-\frac{1}{a_{2}}\left(\mu_{(F)}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\mathfrak{B}\mathfrak{E}^{V}\right),\end{split}

(129)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\frac{B}{2}\overline{\zeta}^{T}=&~\frac{2ia_{1}}{\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\left(\mu+p+\frac{B-\Pi}{2}\right)\Omega^{S}-\frac{ia_{1}}{2\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\left(\mathcal{R}-\tilde{k}^{2}\right)\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}\\
&+\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\mathcal{H}^{T}+\frac{ia_{1}}{a_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\left(\mu_{(F)}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\mathfrak{B}\mathfrak{E}^{V}\right).\end{split}

(130)

VIII.2
Even Sector

The evolution equations for the coefficients in the even sector are

\displaystyle\begin{split}\dot{\mu}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}=&~\left(\frac{\Sigma}{2}-\frac{4\Theta}{3}-\frac{3\Sigma{\mu_{(F)}}}{2B}\right){\mu_{(F)}}^{V}-\frac{3\Sigma{\mu_{(F)}}\mathfrak{B}}{2B}\mathcal{B}^{V}\\
~&+\frac{ia_{1}{\mu_{(F)}}}{2a_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\left(\left(1-\frac{3\Sigma}{B}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\right){\mu_{(F)}}-\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right){\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{S}\\
~&-{\mu_{(F)}}\left(\left(1+\frac{3\Sigma}{2B}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\right){\mu_{(F)}}-\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}\right){\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{V}\\
~&+\frac{3a_{2}\Sigma{\mu_{(F)}}}{2}\left(C-1\right)\mathcal{E}^{T}-\frac{a_{2}B{\mu_{(F)}}}{2}\left(C-1\right)\Sigma^{T}\\
~&-\frac{ia_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}{\mu_{(F)}}}{2a_{1}}\left(J-2\right)\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}-\frac{ia_{1}{\mu_{(F)}}}{\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\left(\frac{G}{B}+\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V}\\
~&+\frac{\mathfrak{B}{\mu_{(F)}}}{2B}\left(2B+3\Sigma\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\right)\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V},\end{split}

(131)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\dot{\mathcal{B}}^{V}=&~\frac{ia_{1}\mathfrak{B}{\mu_{(F)}}}{a_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}B}\left(B-3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\right){\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{S}\\
~&-\frac{3\mathfrak{B}\Sigma}{B}{\mu_{(F)}}^{V}+3a_{2}\mathfrak{B}\Sigma C\mathcal{E}^{T}-a_{2}\mathfrak{B}BC\Sigma^{T}-\frac{ia_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}\mathfrak{B}J}{a_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}\\
~&-\frac{2ia_{1}\mathfrak{B}G}{\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}B}\overline{\Omega}^{V}+\left(\frac{3\Sigma\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{B}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)-\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}\right)\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}\\
~&+\frac{3}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}-\frac{2\Sigma\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{B}\right)\mathcal{B}^{V}-\frac{3\mathfrak{B}\Sigma{\mu_{(F)}}}{B}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right){\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{V},\end{split}

(132)

\displaystyle~\begin{split}\dot{\overline{\mathcal{H}}}^{T}=&-\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}a_{2}B}\left(1-\frac{2a_{2}^{2}\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\right)\left({\mu_{(F)}}^{V}+\mathfrak{B}\mathcal{B}^{V}\right)+\frac{ia_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\mathfrak{B}\mathcal{B}^{V}\\
~&+\frac{2}{a_{2}B}\left(\mu+p+\Pi\right)\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\left(1-\frac{2a_{2}^{2}\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V}\\
~&+\frac{1}{a_{2}^{2}B}\left(1-\frac{2a_{2}^{2}\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\right)\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right){\mu_{(F)}}{\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{S}\\
~&-\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}a_{2}B}\left(1-\frac{2a_{2}^{2}\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\right)\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\left({\mu_{(F)}}{\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{V}-\mathfrak{B}\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}\right)\\
~&+\frac{ia_{1}}{\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\left(\frac{a_{2}^{2}\mathfrak{B}^{2}BL}{3\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}+\frac{3\Pi}{2}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\right)\Sigma^{T}-\left(\frac{2a_{2}^{2}\mathfrak{B}^{2}L}{3\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}+\frac{3}{2}\left(F+\frac{M}{B}\right)\right)\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}\\
~&-\frac{ia_{1}}{\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\left(\frac{3\Pi}{2}+\left(1-C\right)\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}-\frac{\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{a_{2}}\left(\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}B}-\frac{a_{2}^{3}\Sigma L}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\right)\right)\mathcal{E}^{T},\end{split}

(133)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\dot{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{S}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}=&-\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right){\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{S},\end{split}

(134)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\dot{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}=&~\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}-\frac{2\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{\eta{\mu_{(F)}}}\right){\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{V}-\frac{\mathfrak{B}}{\eta{\mu_{(F)}}}\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V},\end{split}

(135)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\dot{\mathcal{E}}^{T}=&-\frac{3}{2}\left(F+\Sigma C\right)\mathcal{E}^{T}+\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{2a_{1}}\left(J-2\right)\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\left(\mu+p-2\Pi\right)\Sigma^{T}+\frac{3\Sigma}{2a_{2}B}\left({\mu_{(F)}}^{V}+\mathfrak{B}\mathcal{B}^{V}\right)+\frac{ia_{1}G}{a_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}B}\overline{\Omega}^{V}\\
&-\frac{ia_{1}}{2a_{2}^{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}B}\left(B-3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\right){\mu_{(F)}}{\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{S}\\
&-\frac{1}{a_{2}B}\left(B-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\right)\left({\mu_{(F)}}{\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{V}-\mathfrak{B}\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}\right),\end{split}

(136)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\dot{\overline{\mathfrak{E}}}^{V}=&~\frac{2a_{2}^{2}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}\mathfrak{B}}{a_{1}^{2}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}B}{\mu_{(F)}}^{V}+\frac{2ia_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}\mathfrak{B}{\mu_{(F)}}}{a_{1}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}B}\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right){\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{S}\\
~&+\left(\frac{2a_{2}^{2}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}{\mu_{(F)}}}{a_{1}^{2}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}B}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)-\frac{1}{\eta}\right)\mathfrak{B}{\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{V}\\
~&+\left(\frac{\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}B}-\frac{a_{2}^{3}\Sigma L}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\right)\mathfrak{B}\mathcal{E}^{T}+\frac{a_{2}^{3}\mathfrak{B}BL}{3\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\Sigma^{T}+\frac{2ia_{2}^{3}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}\mathfrak{B}L}{3a_{1}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}\\
~&+\frac{4ia_{2}^{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}\mathfrak{B}}{a_{1}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}B}\left(\mu+p+\Pi\right)\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V}+\left(1+\frac{a_{2}^{2}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\left(1+\frac{2\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{B}\right)\right)\mathcal{B}^{V}\\
~&-\left(\frac{\Sigma}{2}+\frac{2\Theta}{3}+\frac{1}{\eta}+\frac{2a_{2}^{2}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{a_{1}^{2}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}B}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\right)\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V},\end{split}

(137)

\displaystyle~\dot{\Sigma}^{T}=

\displaystyle~\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\Sigma^{T}-\mathcal{E}^{T},

(138)

\displaystyle~\dot{\overline{\Omega}}^{V}=

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{2\Theta}{3}+\frac{\Sigma}{2}\right)\overline{\Omega}^{V},

(139)

where the additional auxiliary variables are defined as

\displaystyle M=

\displaystyle~2\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{\Pi}{2}\right)\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)+\frac{\Sigma}{a_{2}^{2}}\left(\mathcal{R}a_{2}^{2}-\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}\right),

(140)

\displaystyle~JB=

\displaystyle~\frac{a_{1}^{2}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}^{2}\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}\left(\mathcal{R}-\tilde{k}^{2}\right)-3\Sigma\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right),

(141)

\displaystyle~ G=

\displaystyle~\left(\mu+p+\Pi\right)\left(\mathcal{R}-\tilde{k}^{2}\right),

(142)

\displaystyle~ LB=

\displaystyle~3\Sigma\left(\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}-\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}\right)+\Theta\tilde{k}^{2}.

(143)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}a_{2}^{2}}\phi^{S}=&-\frac{BL}{3}\Sigma^{T}-\frac{2\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}a_{2}B}\left(\mu^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\mathfrak{B}\mathcal{B}^{V}\right)-\frac{2i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}L}{3a_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}\\

(152)

&-\frac{1}{3a_{2}B}\left(3\Sigma\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)-B\right)\left(\mu_{(F)}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}-\mathfrak{B}\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}\right),\end{split}

(156)

\displaystyle~ \begin{split}\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}a_{2}}\alpha^{V}=&-\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathcal{E}+\frac{\Pi}{2}\right)\Sigma^{T}-\frac{3\Sigma}{2a_{2}B}\left(\mu^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}+\mathfrak{B}\mathcal{B}^{V}\right)-\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{2a_{1}}J\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}-\frac{ia_{1}G}{a_{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}B}\overline{\Omega}^{V}\\
&+\frac{3\Sigma C}{2}\mathcal{E}^{T}+\frac{ia_{1}}{2a_{2}^{2}\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}B}\left(B-3\Sigma\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\right)\mu_{(F)}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{S}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\\
&+\frac{1}{a_{2}B}\left(B-\frac{3\Sigma}{2}\left(\Sigma-\frac{2\Theta}{3}\right)\right)\left(\mu_{(F)}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}-\mathfrak{B}\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}\right).\end{split}

(157)

Finally, the only remaining tensorial coefficient is

\frac{i\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}}\zeta^{T}=~\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)\Sigma^{T}-\mathcal{E}^{T}.

(158)

IX
Analyzing the Final System

Since the final equations from the previous section are still rather difficult to deal with analytically, we will mainly consider numerical examples. In doing so, we will focus on analyzing how the perturbations depend on the chosen length scales. However, before moving on to the numerical calculations, we should make some notes about the inherent wave length dependencies introduced when defining the gauge invariant variables and the harmonics.

IX.1
Rescaling the Harmonics

When investigating the dependence on length scale, it is important to note that, due to our definition of the vector and tensor harmonics as gradients of the scalar harmonics, these will naturally contain factors of
k_{\perp}
. Hence, the vector and tensor coefficients will contain inherent factors of
k_{\perp}
relative to the scalar coefficients. Defining a new set of harmonics, where the inherent factors of
k_{\perp}
have been removed,

\displaystyle Q^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k_{\perp}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.59515pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.59515pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.8302pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.05728pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{a_{2}}{\mbox{$k_{\perp}$}}\mathchoice{\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{a}}}}{\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{a}}}}{\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{a}}}}{\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{a}}}}Q^{k_{\perp}},\quad

\displaystyle Q^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k_{\perp}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.59515pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.59515pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.8302pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.05728pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}{b}}}

(159)

\displaystyle\overline{Q}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k_{\perp}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.59515pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.59515pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.8302pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.05728pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{a_{2}^{2}}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.33766pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.33766pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.5901pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.85007pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{c}{\{a}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.33766pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.33766pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.5901pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.85007pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{c}{\{a}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.33766pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.33766pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.5901pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.85007pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{c}{\{a}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.33766pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.33766pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.5901pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.85007pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{c}{\{a}}}}\mathchoice{\delta^{{{c}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\delta^{{{c}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\delta^{{{c}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\delta^{{{c}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{b\}}}}}{\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{b\}}}}}{\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{b\}}}}}{\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.51668pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10292pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.50209pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{b\}}}}}Q^{k_{\perp}}.

(160)

we note that

\displaystyle\Psi^{{{V}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}

\displaystyle=k^{-1}_{\perp}\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},\quad

\displaystyle\overline{\Psi}^{{{V}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}

\displaystyle=k^{-1}_{\perp}\overline{\Psi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},

(161)

\displaystyle\Psi^{{{T}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}

\displaystyle=k^{-2}_{\perp}\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{T}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},\quad

\displaystyle\overline{\Psi}^{{{T}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}

\displaystyle=k^{-2}_{\perp}\overline{\Psi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{T}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},

(162)

where the
\Psi_{*}
are the coefficients relative to the new harmonics. These harmonics have the benefit that the scalar, vector and tensor coefficients do not differ with factors of
k_{\perp}
simply due to the definition of the harmonics.

However, there are still some inherent factors of
k_{\perp}
that need to be addressed. These factors occur for the gauge invariant variables that are defined as gradients of quantities that are non-zero on the background spacetime. If we let
\Phi_{a}=\delta_{a}\Phi
, then the
\delta
gradient implies that the coefficients
\{ \Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},\overline{\Phi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\}
will naturally be one order higher in the factor
\mbox{$k_{\perp}$}/a_{2}
than the coefficients for the corresponding scalar perturbation of
\Phi
. Hence, when investigating the length scale dependencies, we will focus on coefficients of the form
\{\Psi^{S},\Psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V,T}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[14.58209pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.58209pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.18564pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.82306pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},\overline{\Psi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V,T}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[14.58209pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[14.58209pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.18564pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.82306pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},a_{2}k^{-1}_{\perp}\Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}},a_{2}k^{-1}_{\perp}\overline{\Phi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\}
to avoid the aforementioned inherent factors of
k_{\perp}
due to our definitions.

IX.2
Dependence on Perturbation Length Scale

Equipped with the full system of ordinary differential equations and a rescaled set of harmonic coefficients, we now consider specific numerical examples. In these calculations, we will follow a similar convention as in
Bradley et al. 2012
and set
\Lambda=1
, which will fixate the remaining length dimension. As such, we will in the following treat all quantities as being dimensionless. In our calculations we will use the background spacetime specified by the following initial conditions and parameter values

\displaystyle\Sigma(t_{0})

\displaystyle=0,\quad

\displaystyle\mu_{(F)}(t_{0})

\displaystyle=(1-r_{\mathfrak{B}})\mu(t_{0}),\quad

\displaystyle a_{1}(t_{0})

\displaystyle=1,

\displaystyle\Theta(t_{0})

\displaystyle=0.6,\quad

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}(t_{0})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2r_{\mathfrak{B}}\mu(t_{0})},\quad

\displaystyle a_{2}(t_{0})

\displaystyle=1,

(163)

\displaystyle\mu(t_{0})

\displaystyle=0.36,\quad

\displaystyle\gamma

\displaystyle=\frac{5}{3},\quad

where
r_{\mathfrak{B}}
is the fraction between the initial magnetic energy density and the total energy density. The initial conditions for
\mu
,
\Sigma
, and
\Theta
are identical to those for a dust background presented in
Bradley et al. 2012
, which ends at an expanding de Sitter solution. When not stated otherwise, we will use
r_{\mathfrak{B}}=0.1
and the initial resistivity
\eta(t_{0})=10^{-3}
. We will also choose the
k
-space direction
\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}=2\mbox{$k_{\perp}$}=2k(t_{0})/\sqrt{5}
, where

k^{2}=\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}}+\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}^{2}},

(164)

is the square of the norm of the physical wave vector. For all numerical calculations, we solve Eqs. (
52
), (
53
), (
D.1
), (
56
), (
102
)–(
108
), and (
131
)–(
139
) together with Eqs. (
62
), and (
101
). When not stated otherwise, the equations are solved using
ode45
in
MATLAB
with a relative tolerance of
10^{-9}
and an absolute tolerance of
10^{-34}
.

IX.2.1
Tensor Perturbations

As a first example, we consider the generated magnetic fields due to perturbations in some tensorial coefficients. Following a similar line as in
Zunckel et al. 2006
we will let the initial tensorial shear perturbation represent some initial gravitational wave content. The aim is then to investigate how the generated magnetic fields depend on the chosen length scale. Defining the initial Hubble length
L_{H}=3/\Theta(t_{0})
and a characteristic length scale for the perturbations
L=2\pi/k(t_{0})
, we solve the final system of equations for values of
L/L_{H}
in the range
\left[10^{-3},10^{3}\right]
. As for the initial perturbations, we choose

\displaystyle\Sigma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{T}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}(t_{0})=\overline{\Sigma}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{T}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}(t_{0})=10^{-19}k_{m}^{2}\sim 10^{-13},

(165)

or equivalently

\displaystyle\Sigma^{{{T}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}(t_{0})=\overline{\Sigma}^{{{T}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}(t_{0})=10^{-19}\frac{k_{m}^{2}}{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}},

(166)

where
k_{m}
is the maximum value of
k(t_{0})
in the studied range. Requiring
\mathfrak{B}^{V}(t_{0})=0
and using Eqs. (
129
) and (
149
), we also let

\mathcal{H}^{T}(t_{0})=-\frac{ia_{1}B}{2\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\overline{\Sigma}^{T}\Bigg\rvert_{t_{0}},\quad\overline{\mathcal{H}}^{T}(t_{0})=\frac{ia_{1}B}{2\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}}\Sigma^{T}\Bigg\rvert_{t_{0}},

(167)

whilst
\{\Omega^{S}
,
\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{T}
,
{\overline{\mathcal{V}}_{(F)}}^{V}
,
\mathfrak{E}^{S}
,
\mathfrak{E}^{V}
,
\overline{\mathfrak{B}}^{V}
,
\overline{\Omega}^{V}
,
\mathcal{E}^{T}
,
{\mu_{(F)}}^{V}
,
{\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{S}
,
{\mathcal{V}_{(F)}}^{V}
,
\mathcal{B}^{V}
,
\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}\}
are chosen to vanish initially. Hence, in particular, the perturbations of the electromagnetic fields and the plasma velocities are all zero at
t_{0}
. Solving the system, we then get the results shown in Fig.
1
a–c. For super-horizon scales,
L\gg L_{H}
, the curves in the logarithmic plot are linear with a slope close to two. Decreasing
L
below
L_{H}
, the values extracted at the fixed time
t=8
begin to oscillate. In the region around
L/L_{H}=10^{-1}
, the curves displaying the maximum values are almost linear with a slope of approximately one. Eventually when decreasing

even further, it can be seen that the curves approach a constant value.

Figure 1:
Generated magnetic fields as functions of perturbation length scale using the background spacetime specified in Eq. (
163
). Both the maximum amplitude and the values extracted at the integration time
t=8
are shown. In a–c the results using the initial conditions specified in Eqs. (

)–(
167
) are displayed, whilst d–f and g–i uses Eqs. (

)–(

) and Eqs. (

)–(

) respectively. In all calculations,
\mbox{$k_{\parallel}$}=2\mbox{$k_{\perp}$}=2k(t_{0})/\sqrt{5}
, and

. The initial resistivity was
\eta(t_{0})=10^{-3}
in a–f and
\eta(t_{0})=1
in g–h. Sharp and noise-like spikes slightly above
L/L_{H}
equal to unity may be due to numerical problems encountered when the auxiliary function
B
approaches zero.

Finally, it should be remarked that the noise-like spikes at values of
L/L_{H}
slightly larger than unity, as can be seen in Fig.
1
c, are most likely due to numerical problems encountered when the auxiliary variable
B
, defined in Eq. (
110
), passes through zero for
t>t_{0}
. As the final system of ordinary differential equations have been derived under the assumption that
B
is non-zero, a zero-crossing for
B
clearly poses a problem, as the derivatives involving factors of
B^{-1}
are not well defined when
B=0
. In addition to the derivatives not being well defined, we also encounter problems with the coefficients that are given as algebraic expressions in terms of the coefficients in the final system, as their corresponding expressions may involve factors of
B^{-1}
. Therefore, we also have to be careful when specifying initial conditions for the perturbations when
B(t_{0})\sim 0
, since the coefficients with algebraic expressions, which should remain small relative to the background, may become too large even if the initial conditions for the variables in the ODE system appear small.

IX.2.3
Velocity Perturbations With Vanishing Initial Energy Flux

In the previous example, both velocity and electric field perturbations were introduced to ensure that the ohmic currents and magnetic fields vanished initially. However, in doing so we introduce an initial energy flux that
will affect many gravitationally related variables. For instance, this flux appears in both the differential equations and algebraic relations pertaining to the electric and magnetic parts of the Weyl tensor. Hence, the previously chosen initial values may implicitly contain gravitational effects. Therefore, to better isolate the plasma related effects, we also investigate the case where the initial conditions are chosen so that the energy flux vanishes initially. For this purpose we introduce

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}(t_{0})

\displaystyle=\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}(t_{0})=10^{-19}k_{m}

(171)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}^{V}(t_{0})

\displaystyle=-\frac{\mu_{(F)}}{\mathfrak{B}}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\bigg\rvert_{t_{0}},

IX.3
Beat Waves

To further investigate the plasma related effects on subhorizon scales, we perform similar calculations as in Sec.
IX.2.2
, but varying the magnitude of the background magnetic field rather than the length scale of the perturbations. Hence, we introduce the initial conditions

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}(t_{0})

\displaystyle=\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}(t_{0})=10^{-10},

(174)

\displaystyle~\mathfrak{E}^{V}(t_{0})

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{B}\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\big\rvert_{t_{0}},\quad\overline{\mathfrak{E}}^{V}(t_{0})=-\mathfrak{B}\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\big\rvert_{t_{0}},

(175)

but set
\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{S}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}
to zero. To highlight the sought effects, we decrease the initial resistivity drastically to
\eta(t_{0})=10^{-12}
and choose the length scale to be

. Since the smallness of the chosen electrical resistivity appears to make the system quite stiff, we use
ode15s
instead of
ode45
in this section, although without specifying an analytical expression for the Jacobian. As for the initial magnitude of the background magnetic field, this is, for reasons made more clear in the next section, specified through the Alfvén velocity
v_{A}

{v_{A}}^{2}\equiv\cfrac{\cfrac{\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{\mu_{(F)}}}{1+\cfrac{\mathfrak{B}^{2}}{\mu_{(F)}}}.

(176)

On using that

\displaystyle r_{\mathfrak{B}}=\frac{v_{A}^{2}}{2-v_{A}^{2}}\Bigg\rvert_{t_{0}}

(177)

in Eq. (
163
) and calculating for
v_{A}(t_{0})=0.95
, the results in Fig.

are obtained for the tensorial coefficients of the magnetic part of the Weyl tensor. There it can be seen that a clear beat pattern emerges.

and

To get an analytical explanation of this beat pattern, we now seek wave equations for the plasma velocities and the magnetic part of the Weyl tensor in the limit of large wave numbers. Looking at Eq. (
134
), we see that
\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{S}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}
will not give rise to any wave equation. Using Eq. (
62
) and (
134
), we find that the evolution of
\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{S}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}
is simply determined by the scale factors on the background

\displaystyle\frac{\dot{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{S}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{S}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=-\left(\Sigma+\frac{\Theta}{3}\right)=-\frac{\dot{a}_{1}}{a_{1}}\implies\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{S}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}=\frac{\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{S}}}_{{{(F)}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.29308pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.28708pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.3479pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}(t_{0})}{a_{1}},\quad\textnormal{if}\quad a_{1}(t_{0})=1.

(178)

Thus, to obtain any interesting wave equations, we instead look at
\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}
and
\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}
. In doing so, we will assume that the plasma is ideal (
\eta\rightarrow 0
), and that the wave lengths considered are much smaller than the scales on the background, so that

k^{2},\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\parallel}$}}{a_{1}^{2}},\frac{\mbox{$k^{2}_{\perp}$}}{a_{2}^{2}}\gg\Sigma^{2},\Theta^{2},\mathfrak{B}^{2},\mu_{(F)},\mathcal{E}.

(179)

We will then assume that the frequencies for the variables
\mathcal{V}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39798pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.64262pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45903pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{(F)}{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}

and

are of order
k
in this limit, so that, when applying a time derivative on one of these coefficients, the result is one order higher in
k
. The idea is then to truncate the equations, keeping only the terms with the highest orders in
k
. However, to be able to compare the orders of various terms in the equations, we first have to consider the inherent difference in order between the harmonic coefficients.

With the chosen initial conditions in this section,
\Omega^{S},\overline{\Omega}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{V}}}_{{{*}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.44447pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.9472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.81943pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}
and
